 1171=== /Users/scammermike/Downloads/hawking/crates/hawking-core/src/model/qwen38_hybrid_decode.rs [L9728-L9765] ===
```
9728→#[derive(Clone, Debug)]
9729→pub struct Qwen38GenerateResult {
9730→    /// Why generation stopped: "constraint_done", "eos" or "budget".
9731→    ///
9732→    /// A reply that "never closed the JSON object" has three possible causes
9733→    /// and they need different fixes. Empty for the unconstrained path, which
9734→    /// has no constraint to finish.
9735→    pub stop_reason: &'static str,
9736→    pub tokens: Vec<u32>,
9737→    pub prompt_len: usize,
9738→    /// Number of physical command-buffer steps used for prefill. This can be
9739→    /// less than prompt_len when production batching is enabled; keep it
9740→    /// separate so phase attribution does not mistake chunk timing for token
9741→    /// timing.
9742→    pub prefill_step_count: usize,
```

=== /Users/scammermike/Downloads/hawking/crates/hawking-core/src/metal/mod.rs [L684-L691] ===
```
684→    unsafe fn cb_gpu_start_end_s(cb: &metal::CommandBufferRef) -> (Option<f64>, Option<f64>) {
685→        let start: f64 = msg_send![cb, GPUStartTime];
686→        let end: f64 = msg_send![cb, GPUEndTime];
687→        if start > 0.0 && end > start {
688→            (Some(start), Some(end))
689→        } else {
690→            (None, None)
691→        }
```

=== /Users/scammermike/Downloads/hawking/crates/hawking-core/src/metal/mod.rs [L6416-L6421] ===

```
6418→        /// ns ledger. Decode output is unchanged.
6419→        pub fn commit_and_wait_timed(self) -> Result<super::CommandBufferTiming> {
6420→            self.commit_and_wait_split()
6421→        }
```

```
6634→                let (gpu_start, gpu_end) = unsafe { cb_gpu_start_end_s(&cmd) };

6637→                    timing.gpu_end_ns = Some((end * 1_000_000_000.0) as u64);
6638→                    if end > start {

6642→                    }
```
